E436 | COMPETITORS-ONT | Oxford Nanopore H1'26: revenue GBP 116.7M vs 105.6M (+12.3% constant currency), gross margin 62.2% vs 58.2%, PromethION +15.7% (P2i-driven). Management credits yield improvements across flow cells, scale and 'increased adoption of the new pricing model'. ONT's margin rose while it changed pricing, the opposite of the price-versus-margin pressure at PacBio (E421). Price per Gb and tier detail are still unobtained. | FACT / MGMT | Oxford Nanopore H1 2026 results via search summary, Aug 2026, directorstalkinterviews.com and nanoporetech.com H1-26 presentation | corroboration: 1 | (wave agents 129, 130, 131, 132)
CORRECTION (129, 130, 131, 132): E420 : The DoD 1260H list updated on 9 Jun 2026 names BGI Group plus 7 affiliates, MGI Tech and Novogene, per Mondaq. It does not name BGI Genomics separately and does not mention Complete Genomics. Novogene is a large Illumina-heavy service provider (E360, E399) and is not in E420. A search snippet saying Complete Genomics is listed is unverified and conflicts with Mondaq and E420. The OMB list is due 18 Dec 2026 and prohibitions are 'likely sometime in 2027' (Mondaq 2026-06-09, https://www.mondaq.com/china/life-sciences-biotechnology-nanotechnology/1801038/biosecure-update-1260h-list-released).
CORRECTION (129, 130, 131, 132): E082 : The '>=250 Gb' figure for Personalis NeXT Personal appears to belong to NeXT Liquid Biopsy (>=250 Gb, ~5,000x boosted genes, ~2,000x exome-wide), a different product. NeXT Personal is tumor/normal WGS for up to ~1,800 variants, followed by a personalized plasma panel (LOD95 3.45 ppm), and its plasma Gb is undisclosed. Treat the E082 figure as unverified (Personalis release 2024-03-26, https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20240326574824/en/Personalis-Announces-Publication-Validating-NeXT-Personal-Test-for-Ultra-sensitive-MRD-Detection-and-Cancer-Treatment-Response-Monitoring). E083's WGS-MRD high case and E087 should be read with that caveat.
CORRECTION (129, 130, 131, 132): E414 : The Twist 48% top-10 customer figure is still unverified. A fetch of the FQ3'26 10-Q (acc. 0001581280-26-000047) returned only part of the filing, with no concentration table. The figure rests solely on the MarketBeat call summary, so lower confidence (agent 130).
CORRECTION (129, 130, 131, 132): E082/E099 : Amplicon MRD test growth of +45-60% converts to much less Gb growth, because the one-off tumor exome carries roughly 80-90% of the Gb per patient-year and the serial draws only 10-20% (OPINION, derived from the Natera spec and the IMvigor011/GALAXY cadence). Use this when converting MRD test growth to Gb.
E437 | PRICING-EU-TENDER | Fundació de Recerca Clínic Barcelona-IDIBAPS (project PMPER24/00011, ISCIII/NextGenerationEU funded) awarded 13 NovaSeq X 25B 300-cycle kits for EUR 208,000 ex-VAT on 5 Jun 2025, which is EUR 16,000 per kit. The estimate was EUR 249,600 (EUR 19,200 per kit), so the award came in 16.7% below it. The award went to Diagnostica Longwood S.L., an Illumina distributor, not to Illumina directly. Award criteria were 50% price and 50% quality. At an assumed ~8,000 Gb per 25B cell (agent assumption, not re-verified) this is about EUR 2.00/Gb awarded and EUR 2.40/Gb at the estimate. That is about 20% above the CIBER-implied bound (E433) and below the CNAG service rate (E291). Awarded public-tender prices sit at or below the ~$2.00/Gb launch list. It is the first actual award, not an estimate, for an X flow cell in the ledger, and it came via a distributor, so it is not Illumina's direct net price. | FACT (award) / OPINION (EUR/Gb, assumed Gb per cell) | Publications Office of the EU procurement summary (published 2025-04-11, awarded 2025-06-05, fetched 2026-10-11), https://op.europa.eu/en/web/public-procurement/procurement-details/-/procurement/21279da3-5c1f-491d-8c7b-e21c35d651aa | corroboration: 1 | (wave agents 133, 134, 135, 136)
E438 | Q2-VOLUME | Signatera Genome is 64 clonal variants at up to 350,000x each, about 22M reads, against ~1.6M reads for classic Signatera (16 amplicons at ~100,000x). Converted at 100-150 bp reads, that is about 2.2-3.4 Gb per plasma draw against 0.16-0.24 Gb for classic Signatera, roughly 10-15x more per draw. Natera launched it broadly on 2025-04-24. This is the first quantified plasma-Gb step-up for an installed WGS-style MRD product. The 350,000x and 64-variant figures come from a search snippet of the release, not the fetched page, so the Gb conversion is an agent derivation. It refines E082/E432, which cover only the classic assay and say '<1 Gb'. | FACT (spec, snippet-level) / OPINION (Gb conversion) | Natera/BusinessWire 2025-04-24, https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20250424920561/en/Natera-Announces-Broad-Clinical-Launch-of-Ultra-Sensitive-Signatera-Genome-MRD-Test ; NICE MIB307 | corroboration: 1 | (wave agents 133, 134, 135, 136)
E439 | READ-THROUGH-MRD-COVERAGE | Medicare covers Signatera Genome under MolDX LCD L38779 for CRC, breast, bladder, ovarian and lung cancers, plus pan-cancer immunotherapy monitoring. CRC monitoring is allowed 'with the same frequency as CEA' (announced 2025-06-04). CEA surveillance runs every 3-6 months (agent background knowledge, not verified), which implies about 2-4 draws a year. This ties the frequency cap to an existing reimbursement rule and is consistent with the GALAXY cadence of ~4 draws a year (E428). It is the most useful sourced limit on draws per patient in CRC, so it limits cadence-driven Gb upside in CRC. | FACT (coverage wording) / OPINION (cadence) | Natera/BusinessWire 2025-06-04, https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20250604942709/en ; GenomeWeb | corroboration: 1 | (wave agents 133, 134, 135, 136)
E440 | READ-THROUGH-MRD-COVERAGE | The ASCO 2026 'CIRCULATE' result of 62% vs 19% 3-year recurrence is CIRCULATE-AIO (NCT04089631, Germany/Austria, academic tumor-informed assay, stage II MSS colon), not CIRCULATE-US and not Signatera. ctDNA positivity was only 2.9% of randomized patients, from a single post-op draw at a median of 21 days. Sensitivity was 31% and specificity 98%. The 62%/19% figures are per-protocol (21 chemo vs 15 observation patients). The intention-to-treat primary analysis was formally negative because of under-enrollment. CIRCULATE-US (NRG-GI008, Signatera, serial monitoring in ctDNA-negative patients) had 967 of 1,912 planned patients on 4 Sep 2025 and has no readout. Neither adds serial-draw support, and the AIO trial tests a rare, single-draw use case. This extends E429/E430 and removes a likely mislabel. | FACT | OncLive 2026-05-31, https://www.onclive.com/view/ctdna-guided-adjuvant-chemotherapy-improves-dfs-reduces-disease-recurrence-in-stage-ii-colon-cancer ; NRG Oncology/Duke listings (via search) | corroboration: 1 | (wave agents 133, 134, 135, 136)
E441 | MGMT-CONDUCT | The 2026 DEF 14A, per search snippets, describes Illumina's AIP as a formula plan with pre-established revenue and non-GAAP operating income goals, giving 77% funding for 2025. These metrics are revenue and non-GAAP operating income in dollars, not operating margin. This resolves the label question in the E220 correction. No clinical or NovaSeq X metric appears in the snippets, and the thresholds, weights and actuals are still unread. The 2025 PSUs vest half on 3-year operating margin and half on relative TSR against Nasdaq Biotech companies, with the period ending 2 Jan 2028. The margin hurdle will not be disclosed until the cycle ends. | FACT (snippet-level) | Illumina DEF 14A acc. 0001193125-26-149669, https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1110803/000119312526149669/d13789ddef14a.htm (search snippet, 2026-10-11) | corroboration: 1 | (wave agents 133, 134, 135, 136)
E442 | COMPETITORS-PACBIO | On PacBio's Q2'26 call (reported 2026-08-05), Stephens' Mason Carrico asked whether cost reductions lead to higher sample volumes that offset lower pricing. CEO Van Oene suggested 'customer demand and project scale should close the revenue gap'. He did not claim volume is already offsetting the cut. This is the same price-versus-volume question as the ILMN thesis, and a challenger CEO answered it with an expectation rather than evidence. It fits the weak PacBio consumables growth of +6% and the margin drop to 36% (E421). The source is a question summary, not a verified transcript. | MGMT (summary-level) | StockStory/FinancialContent 2026-08-12, https://www.financialcontent.com/article/stockstory-2026-8-12-5-must-read-analyst-questions-from-pacbios-q2-earnings-call | corroboration: 1 | (wave agents 133, 134, 135, 136)
E443 | MARKET-SIZING | ACS Facts & Figures 2026 estimates 2,114,850 new US cancer cases and 626,140 deaths. Colorectal accounts for 158,850 new cases (108,860 colon, 49,990 rectal). These are estimates, not observed counts, and have no stage split. They give a sourced denominator for an MRD addressable-patient model, but eligible-patient share, adoption and resection rates are still assumptions. | FACT | cancer.org 2026, https://www.cancer.org/content/dam/cancer-org/research/cancer-facts-and-statistics/annual-cancer-facts-and-figures/2026/mr6-leading-sites-2026-branded.pdf ; https://www.cancer.org/cancer/types/colon-rectal-cancer/about/key-statistics.html | corroboration: 1 | (wave agents 133, 134, 135, 136)
CORRECTION (133, 134, 135, 136): E420 / CORRECTION (129-132) : Reverse the claim that Complete Genomics is not on the 1260H list. WilmerHale's alert (full text fetched, 2026-06-11) says DoD added Complete Genomics, Inc. in the June 2026 update. It lists 'BGI Americas Corporation; Complete Genomics, Inc.; GBI Diagnostics, Inc.; Innomics Inc.; and STOmics Americas Ltd.' as BGI Group subsidiary additions. The GenomeWeb headline ('...Include Novogene, Complete Genomics, Others') and a Ropes & Gray snippet agree. Mondaq's 'BGI Group + 7 affiliates' likely includes it. Novogene is a parent-level listing. This closes the US-domiciled route for Complete Genomics, so Chinese-platform switching in US clinical labs is slightly less likely before 2028. The Federal Register notice 2026-11571 was not opened (WilmerHale, https://www.wilmerhale.com/en/insights/client-alerts/20260611-pentagon-adds-65-new-entities-to-the-1260h-list-of-chinese-military-companies).

WilmerHale does not name MGI Tech, while Ropes & Gray and Mondaq do. MGI's status is probably a parent-level listing and remains unverified against the Federal Register.
CORRECTION (133, 134, 135, 136): E082 / E432 : '<1 Gb per plasma draw' holds only for classic Signatera (~0.16-0.24 Gb). Signatera Genome (64 variants, up to 350,000x) is about 2.2-3.4 Gb per draw (Natera/BusinessWire 2025-04-24, snippet-level). A shift of MRD mix to Genome-type tests therefore raises plasma Gb per draw by about 10x, on top of the one-off tumor/normal Gb. NeXT Personal plasma Gb remains undisclosed.
CORRECTION (133, 134, 135, 136): E131 : MGI selling Complete Genomics to Swiss Rockets for $50M sits alongside the 1260H listing of Complete Genomics, Inc. in Jun 2026. The sale's closing date is unchecked, so the listing may be stale or contested if the sale has closed (WilmerHale 2026-06-11; E332).
E444 | Q2-VOLUME / Q1-OVEREARNING | GeneDx Q2'26 10-Q: one unnamed supplier of reagents, equipment and supplies was ~14% of spend in Q2'26 vs ~20% in Q2'25. E445 | Q1-OVEREARNING | Consensus-implied clinical Gb growth, break-even grid. Anchor: Q2'26 HT $535M, X $316M, 6000 $219M, with 83%/17% of HT Gb on X/6000. Assumes clinical is 64% of HT Gb and 6000 conversion of 12.5% per quarter. Holding Q3'26 consensus ($789M) needs ~31% clinical Gb growth at a flat X price and ~47% at -10%/yr. Q4'26 ($815M) needs ~30-51%, depending on the 14th-week value. FY27 consensus ($3,378M, +8.8%) needs HT Gb of ~35% at flat price or ~53% at -10%/yr, i.e. ~50-77% clinical. A 20/25/30% clinical grid gives only ~$2.86-2.97B for FY27. This puts a number on what consensus embeds and favors E205 over E083. | OPINION (calc, +/-1% rounding, hand-computed) | file 140 section 1 (internal model off ledger inputs E040/E050), 2026-10-11 | corroboration: 1 | (wave agents 137, 138, 139, 140)

E448 | Q1-OVEREARNING | Q4'26 consensus on a 13-week basis. If the 14th week is worth $0, $23M (the call's '0.5 point') or the full 1/13, the $815M Q4 bar becomes $815M, $792M or $757M. Against the Q3 bar of $789M that is +3%, flat or -4% sequentially. E449 | MGMT | Language arc on 'headwind' (Thaysen). Feb 2026 (Q4'25 call): blended consumables 'still face headwinds'. Apr (Q1'26): the price headwind is 'receding'. E450 | Q2-VOLUME | Q3'26 call checklist with model-derived thresholds. Over-earning is confirmed if consumables are below $775M, derived 6000 revenue is at or above $219M (not running off), X revenue share is below 60% or volume is stuck at 83%, ex-China clinical growth is below 12% (comp +12%), or pull-through is below $1.20M annualized. A 35B list cut of 15% or more on existing customers would be worth ~$150M of FY27 at an assumed 50% scope. It is refuted if the figures are $789M or more, 6000 revenue of $213M or less, 61%+ revenue share, or 13-16% clinical growth. Dropped disclosures (pull-through guide, 83%/59% slide) are a flag. | OPINION | file 140 section 4, 2026-10-11 | corroboration: 1 | (wave agents 137, 138, 139, 140)

Needs a read-count source before E082 is used. Also E082's exome ~8 Gb and tissue CGP ~15 Gb sit above Caris WES+WTS at ~5-10 Gb combined (own estimate), and this is unresolved.
